# Week06 人车交互模型与实验完整记录

整理日期：2026-10-23。综合文档版。

本文件合并模型、规则、参数、全部灵敏度汇总、社会力对照、失败与修正、668次运行的单次指标、测试记录及可运行源码。详细文档在docs，交互回放、图片、数据和代码保留；旧沿环结果已删除，首页为向心穿越回放。本文件可独立阅读，逐时刻轨迹与动画也可由附录源码重新生成。

668次包含360次统一规则灵敏度、120次三模型配对对照、48次SFM参数探索、11个失败/修正案例、9个基础演示、120次向心穿越。演示可能重复相同配置，总数不代表668个独立样本。圆环为人车交替排布、向心穿越至对径点，采用各自主方向坐标和固定朝向矩形；参数未用实测数据标定。

## 第1部分：实验报告与主要结论

生成日期：2026-10-23。所有数值来自已完成的仿真输出；本文保留模型、结果与可复现代码。

### 1. 交付内容与主要发现

本次整理了 668 次已保存仿真：统一规则灵敏度 360 次、三模型配对对照 120 次、社会力参数探索 48 次、失败与修正 11 次、基础场景演示 9 次、向心穿越 120 次。演示和对照可能重复相同配置，不能将总数解释为相互独立样本。

- 人和车共享运动方程及交互实现，区别只体现在尺寸和运动能力参数；质量不直接分配避让责任。

- 默认车宽1.8 m、人宽0.6 m，几何下限为2.4 m；加入双方间距0.2 m和两侧各0.1 m后，安全断面下限为2.8 m。

- 默认3人2车、5个种子：5.0 m道路平均完成 43.88 s，3.2 m为 48.34 s，2.8 m为 76.30 s；2.6 m全部等待。

- 2.9 m道路即使总宽够，车辆固定居中仍会挡住通道；允许车辆以0.2 m/s上限小幅靠边后通过。

- 纯社会力基线在部分宽路能完成，但在窄路、多方向汇聚中出现碰撞/越界/僵持；逐步修正对照保留了未成功的中间方案。

- 圆环实验为人车交替排布、向中心穿越到对径点；默认4人4车形成僵持，单人单车规则模型可完成。

模型方程与假设（已纳入本文，相关文件也可重新运行生成） · 规则与指标定义（已纳入本文，相关文件也可重新运行生成） · 参数表（已纳入本文，相关文件也可重新运行生成） · 运行方法（已纳入本文，相关文件也可重新运行生成）


### 2. 基础设置与实验口径

直路长60 m，默认3人2车，车辆沿+s、行人沿−s。所有主体初始同一横向路径，车辆根据最窄宽度预先靠边。向心实验默认半径12 m、4人4车，观察120 s。步长0.05 s，每个正式配置种子0–4；随机性仅为速度基值的±8%扰动。

三模型使用同一初始状态和约束，不按类型调用不同积分器。纯SFM参数A=8 m/s²、B=1.5 m，软墙幅值3 m/s²、范围0.2 m。它是同包络适配的工程基线，未做实测标定；有限参数探索结果全部公开。

通过时间只对已完成直路运行求均值。失败、等待和超时用—表示，不补零。向心实验以对径目标到达数和全部完成率评价，不使用周期通量。

### 6. 失败案例、原因与修正对照

| 案例 | 策略 | 终局 | 完成/s | 互过次数 | 碰撞对数 | 越界主体数 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 空间不足：2.6 m 等待 | 统一规则 | 安全间距不足等待 | — | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| 扩大宽度：2.8 m 通过 | 统一规则 | 全部通过 | 76.82 | 6 | 0 | 0 |
| 车居中：2.9 m 阻塞 | 统一规则 | 固定位置阻塞 | — | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| 允许车侧移：2.9 m 通过 | 统一规则 | 全部通过 | 64.36 | 6 | 0 | 0 |
| 制动距离不足：初速 12 m/s | 统一规则 | 碰撞 | — | 0 | 1 | 0 |
| 只降低初始/期望车速：1.5 m/s | 统一规则 | 全部通过 | 35.45 | 1 | 0 | 0 |
| SFM：短作用距离导致风险 | 社会力基线 | 碰撞 | — | 0 | 1 | 0 |
| SFM：扩大作用范围，5 m 通过 | 社会力基线 | 全部通过 | 80.21 | 6 | 0 | 0 |
| SFM：3.2 m 窄路软边界不足 | 社会力基线 | 越界 | — | 0 | 0 | 1 |
| 部分修正：选边与制动，仍可能僵持 | 社会力+部分修正 | 策略僵持 | — | 3 | 0 | 0 |
| 完整修正：解除已消除的冲突力 | 社会力+完整修正 | 全部通过 | 54.12 | 6 | 0 | 0 |

- 空间不足：2.6 m无法同时保留所设安全裕度。改为2.8 m后通过；没有通过压缩实体或取消碰撞检查来伪造修正。

- 固定车道阻塞：2.9 m车居中时单侧空间不够；只开放车辆小幅侧移能力后通过。

- 制动距离不足：初始中心距5 m、车速12 m/s时发生碰撞；保持位置及其他条件，只将初始/期望车速降为1.5 m/s后通过。它说明控制器无法补救任意危险初态。

- 社会力作用过短：A=4、B=0.8的5 m场景发生碰撞；A=8、B=1.5在该宽路配置通过。但增加排斥范围并不能解决所有窄路问题。

- 软边界不足：相同SFM参数在3.2 m发生越界。加入明确横向目标和制动后虽避免此失败，保留已分离主体的排斥仍可造成后续僵持。

- 中间修正失败：sfm_guided_ungated互过3次后僵持；在已经横向分离的相向主体间解除SFM排斥，完整修正版完成6次互过并全部退出。向心实验另列为多方向汇聚对照。

这些解释对应保存的案例，不应泛化为所有新场景的充分因果证明。断面诊断是必要条件分析，几何上能放下不意味着局部策略一定可达。

探索场景为3.2 m、1人1车、种子100–102。默认A=8、B=1.5参考了探索结果；未使用实测数据，也未声称对所有路宽、密度和软墙参数完成调优。

### 8. 验证与复现

自动测试覆盖类型/质量不变性、同步顺序不变性、加减速限制、几何临界值、固定车道对照、缩窄路、连续步内穿越碰撞、固定朝向矩形交叉接触、整体旋转不变性、向心终点停车、SFM失败和混合模型修正。完整输出见 验证日志（已纳入本文，相关文件也可重新运行生成）。

核心仿真仅依赖Python标准库。统计图可导出PNG和SVG，回放不依赖网络。本文保留单次运行指标；重跑可生成包含参数、初态和轨迹的JSON/CSV。

```powershell

python -m unittest discover -s tests -v

python -m pvsim all --seeds 5 --plots --out results


```

### 9. 仍需验证的内容

- 使用实际人车轨迹标定期望速度、反应/感知延迟、距离感知、个体差异和软墙参数。

- 真正车辆转向与刚体弯道：当前小幅侧移是受限二维速度模型，向心主体保持初始朝向，尚无自行车模型或转向角约束。

- 局部等待与全局可达性：当前只检测全体停滞；可加入等待图、退让、交替放行、避车港和全局路径规划。

- 更多重复与不确定性来源：每配置5次仅覆盖有限的速度扰动；Wilson区间很宽，不能把5/5等同真实世界可靠率100%。

- 初始速度扫描同时改变期望速度，初始距离扫描也改变剩余路程。原始表保留这些设计，不能把完成时间差全部归因于碰撞规避效率。

- [Yang et al.：人车多状态社会力交互框架](https://arxiv.org/abs/2005.07769)

## 第2部分：模型方程与假设

### 1. 统一状态和动力学

每个主体 i 使用同一个状态 `z_i = (s_i, y_i, u_i, w_i)`：s 是纵向坐标，y 是横向坐标，u ≥ 0 是沿意图方向的速度，w 是横向速度；方向 d_i ∈ {−1,+1}。主体用长 l_i、宽 b_i 的轴对齐矩形包络表示，行人也使用保守矩形包络。车辆不作航向旋转。

共享的更新方程是：

```text
u_i(k+1) = clip[控制器给出的目标速度,
                max(0, u_i(k) − brake_i Δt), u_i(k) + accel_i Δt]
w_i(k+1) = 满足横向速度与横向加速度上限的控制量
s_i(k+1) = s_i(k) + d_i u_i(k+1) Δt
y_i(k+1) = y_i(k) + w_i(k+1) Δt
```

这是半隐式 Euler 离散化；每一步内位置按新速度线性移动。所有主体先读取同一时刻快照计算控制，再一起更新。制动能力不足时只按最大减速度刹车，仍可能碰撞；不会把位置投影到安全处，也不会瞬时置零速度。

车辆横向上限设为 0，就保持初始车道；设为 0.2 m/s，即允许缓慢靠边。行人默认 0.7 m/s。两类主体仍调用完全相同的 `ReciprocalPolicy.control`。

### 2. 统一规则交互模型

模型由期望运动、选边/路径保持、互惠制动包络和受限积分组成，是本实验构造的透明基线，不是完整 ORCA，也不是已经标定的社会力模型。

**期望运动。** 纵向速度向期望速度以 `speed_tau` 松弛。相向主体进入交互范围后，目标速度还受到交会速度限制。横向目标取可预见窄断面中的指定侧安全边界，控制 `w_des = clip[(y_target − y)/tau_y, −w_max, +w_max]`，并用横向停止距离限制接近目标的速度。默认行人选 +y 侧，车选 −y 侧；固定选边减少左右振荡。

**速度随宽度变化。** 设可用余量 `q = W_look − b_i − b_j − 2 margin_wall − gap_lateral`，交会速度系数为：

```text
f(q) = f_min + (1 − f_min) clip(q / q_comfort, 0, 1)
u_target ≤ encounter_speed × f(q)
```

默认 f_min = 0.35，q_comfort = 0.60 m。它是明确加入的“窄路慢行”行为假设。因此临界宽度附近变慢不是独立于假设得到的普适定律。宽路目标侧移更远，路径长度也可能增加，结果不必对宽度严格单调。

**冲突判断。** 当横向包络净间距 `|y_i − y_j| − (b_i+b_j)/2` 达到指定侧向间距时，解除相向纵向停车约束。使用当前与一小步外推的较小横向间距，避免仅凭侧移意图提前放行。实际接触仍由独立几何模块检测。

**互惠制动。** 对尚未横向分离的相向主体，令纵向前向包络间隔减去静态间距后的剩余距离为 D，双方各分配 λD，λ = 1/2。控制上限满足：

```text
u_i T + u_i² / (2 brake_i) ≤ λ max(D, 0)
u_safe = sqrt[(brake_i T)² + 2 brake_i λ max(D,0)] − brake_i T
T = reaction_buffer + Δt
```

`reaction_buffer` 是制动规划余量，不是已实现的感知延迟。遇到多个冲突取各上限的最小值。同向跟随使用前车静止的保守上限（λ=1），不假设前车必定继续运动。控制最终仍受最大制动能力约束，所以上限不构成任意初态下的无碰撞证明。

**质量。** 质量保留为元数据，不直接产生“轻者必须让重者”的规则。双方行为差异由尺寸、速度、纵横向机动能力和制动能力表达。本版 A、制动上限等都直接以加速度给定；若未来使用实际驱动力 F_max，可以通过 a_max=F_max/m 引入质量，而不是把质量当作路权。

### 3. 三种可通行条件

1. 几何极限：`W_geom = b_vehicle + b_pedestrian`。默认 1.8+0.6 = 2.4 m；等号是零裕度贴合极限，不代表严格无接触通行。
2. 安全断面：`W_safe = b_vehicle + b_pedestrian + 2 margin_wall + gap_lateral`。默认 2.8 m。
3. 给定位置和运动能力可达：若车固定在 y_v，指定行人侧剩余宽度仍须容纳行人和间距。对居中不动的车，单侧条件为 `W ≥ b_vehicle + 2 b_pedestrian + 2 margin_wall + 2 gap_lateral`，默认 3.6 m。

主宽度扫描为 5.0→2.4 m，不低于默认几何下限。安全下限以下可能仍几何可放下，却应在当前安全参数下等待。

这些是断面必要条件，不能证明沿整个道路存在可实现路径。缩窄道路还取决于会车位置、进入顺序、是否允许倒退和是否有避车区；本版不倒退、不借用路外空间。

### 4. 社会力基线与修正

社会力基线采用期望速度松弛、主体排斥和道路边界排斥的常见结构：

```text
a_i = (v_des − v_i)/tau_i
      + Σ_j A exp(−g_ij/B) ω_ij n_ij
      + a_wall
ω_ij = λ_aniso + (1−λ_aniso)(1+cos φ_ij)/2
```

`n_ij` 为中心连线方向。为了与规则模型使用相同实体尺寸，沿该方向用矩形 Minkowski 包络的有效半径替代圆盘半径：`r_eff = min(h_x/|n_x|, h_y/|n_y|)`，`g_ij = center_distance − r_eff`。这不是矩形距离的精确梯度，是明确的包络适配。两侧墙使用指数排斥；只考虑本处宽度，未自带前瞻路径规划。

SFM 同样受速度、加速、制动、横向速度和横向加速度限制，采用相同同步积分器及连续接触检测。A 的单位为 m/s²，B 为 m。本次使用 A=8、B=1.5 作为工程默认值，并保留 A∈{2,4,8,16}、B∈{0.4,0.8,1.5,2.5} 的参数探索；没有实测标定。默认值参考了单人单车种子100的探索结果，参数表公开全部探索结果，不能把它称为盲测优化结果。

三组正式比较：

| 策略 | 纵向交互 | 横向交互 | 已消除冲突的处理 |
|---|---|---|---|
| reciprocal | 期望速度与制动包络 | 显式选边和路径保持 | 横向分离后解除停车约束 |
| social_force | 期望速度与指数排斥 | 期望路径与指数排斥 | 保留距离排斥 |
| sfm_guided | SFM 纵向速度与规则上限取小 | 使用规则规划的横向控制 | 横向已分离的相向主体不再施加 SFM 排斥 |

另有 `sfm_guided_ungated`：加入选边和制动，但没有解除已消除的排斥，作为中间修正失败对照。修正版明确引入了额外规划和约束，不再是纯 SFM。实验只比较这些具体实现与参数，不能推断所有社会力模型都不如规则模型。

### 5. 圆环向心穿越

人车交替均布于圆周，同时向圆心出发，目标是对径点。默认4人4车、R=12 m、观察120 s。控制器在各自主方向的局部坐标中复用，真实运动使用二维向量合成；固定朝向矩形使用连续SAT接触检测。没有环形道路和循环行驶。

近邻的不同朝向包络以投影矩形适配原控制器，可能导致保守停车；侧移目标取各自局部+n方向2.5 m。车辆初始朝向固定，尚未模拟真实转向。详见向心模型、参数及结果（已纳入本文，相关文件也可重新运行生成）。

### 6. 文献定位

- Helbing & Molnár, *Social Force Model for Pedestrian Dynamics*, Physical Review E 51 (1995)：期望运动与社会排斥的经典结构。https://arxiv.org/abs/cond-mat/9805244
- Yang, Redmill & Ozguner (2020), *A Multi-State Social Force Based Framework for Vehicle-Pedestrian Interaction in Uncontrolled Pedestrian Crossing Scenarios*：人车相互影响与可替换驾驶策略的相关框架。https://arxiv.org/abs/2005.07769
- van den Berg et al., ORCA：互惠避碰的相关路线；本代码没有实现 ORCA 半平面优化，也不继承其保证。https://gamma.cs.unc.edu/ORCA/

## 第3部分：更新规则与评价指标

### 每个时间步

1. 对所有未退出主体读取同一个旧状态快照；向心场景先变换到主体各自局部坐标。
2. 同一策略根据期望运动、道路、相对位置和主体能力产生控制量。主模型和 SFM 不通过 `kind` 判断行人或车辆。
3. 受限积分得到下一状态；不覆盖速度上限不足所产生的真实制动过程。
4. 检查整个线性位移段的矩形接触、最近间距，以及分段线性道路边界。向心场景对不同朝向矩形使用连续SAT检测。
5. 记录碰撞、越界、互相通过、出口离开/对径目标到达等事件。
6. 达到退出、失败、持续停滞或观察时限条件后停止，输出原始轨迹与指标。

### 交互规则

- 默认车向 +s、人向 −s；人选 +y 侧、可侧移车辆选 −y 侧。车辆横向能力为零时保持初始位置。
- 相向近邻使用固定侧目标，直到对方尾部及纵向间距都已经通过，才恢复初始路径。多个来车持续保持交互状态。
- 未实现动态路权、倒退、掉头、外部信号、停车港湾或智能体之间的显式协商。因此无解时等待是允许的结果。
- 默认车辆起点根据最窄宽度预先靠边，**宽度实验不是“车永远居中”**。居中不动与允许侧移另外对照，避免把额外占位限制混入几何下限。
- SFM 的软墙不等于硬约束。越界即记录失败，不能通过裁剪位置掩盖。

### 状态和终止分类

| 分类 | 条件/解释 |
|---|---|
| completed | 直路中所有主体尾部离开目标端；向心场景中全部进入对径目标容差并停稳 |
| collision | 连续步内检测到两个物理矩形包络接触或重叠，接触也计为碰撞 |
| boundary_violation | 物理包络离开道路边界 |
| waiting_geometry | 全体未退出主体持续低速，且最窄断面不满足几何必要条件 |
| waiting_clearance | 全体持续低速，几何可放下但安全断面条件不满足 |
| waiting_alignment | 全体持续低速，安全总宽足够但固定主体的位置阻塞指定侧通道 |
| deadlock_policy | 全体持续低速，以上断面条件未解释停滞；需查看运动能力、选边、排斥力与局部目标 |
| timeout | 开放道路达到观察时限仍未全部退出，不能等同于死锁 |

停滞判据：所有未退出主体二维速度模长小于 0.025 m/s，持续 8 s。短暂停车后恢复不算死锁，持续横移也不算停滞。当前是全体停滞检测，不能保证发现“部分主体永久等待、其他主体仍在动”的所有情况。

诊断由几何条件分层给出，属于候选原因解释。尤其对任意新道路或纯 SFM，不能把断面检查直接当作因果证明。数据中同时保留间距、位置、事件和策略名，以便复查。

### 指标

| 字段 | 单位与含义 |
|---|---|
| completion_time_s | 最后一个主体尾部离开时间；未完成为 null |
| observation_time_s | 仿真实际观察时长；等待/碰撞时不等于完成时间 |
| pedestrians_passed / vehicles_passed | 开放道路中完成退出的唯一主体数 |
| pair_passages | 相向主体尾部相互错开且保留纵向间隔的事件数；仅用于直路对照 |
| min_pair_gap_m | 整个步内所有物理包络的最小欧氏间距；不是单独的横向间距 |
| min_wall_gap_m | 所有运动段内主体到墙的最小净间距 |
| safety_margins_satisfied | 无碰撞越界，且最小物理间距和边界间距满足设定裕度（含 1e−5 m 数值容差） |
| total_agent_waiting_s | 各主体低速等待时间之和，是“主体·秒”，可以大于仿真时长 |
| speed_ceiling_unreachable_agent_steps | 当前保守目标上限无法在一步内达到的主体步数，不等同于必然碰撞 |
| mean_longitudinal_speed_m_s | 已观察时段中平均纵向进展速度；跨不同失败时刻比较需谨慎 |

开放道路输出的每分钟通过数是有限初始批次的观察值，不能直接称为稳态道路容量。主要报告完成率、条件完成时间、间距和停滞分类。

### 公平对照与边界

三种模型使用同一场景对象的深拷贝：道路、尺寸、初始位置、速度、加减速上限、横向能力、步长、碰撞检测、退出定义和停滞判据相同。每个配置采用相同种子0–4，随机性只来自期望/初始速度的±8%独立扰动。

纯 SFM 没有主模型的显式窄路慢行、选边和制动包络；这是待比较的策略差异，不是积分器差异。它也没有数据标定或完备的路径规划。因此结果支持“本实现的行为差异和修正效果”，不支持现实交通安全性能、所有社会力模型排名或一般性的无碰撞保证。

### 向心场景差异

向心场景以对径目标到达数评价通过，完成的主体保留为静止实体。没有物理道路边界；不使用断面宽度等待标签、圈数和周期通量。碰撞检测为固定朝向OBB连续SAT。`min_pair_gap_m`是全步间距保守下界，不与直路精确欧氏间距直接比较。未完成时间仍为null；120 s超时与全体低速持续8 s的僵持分开记录。模型的局部坐标投影是保守适配，见CIRCLE.md（已纳入本文，相关文件也可重新运行生成）。

## 第4部分：参数表

除文献模型结构外，下列数值均为本原型的工程假设；没有以现场数据标定。单位统一为 m、s、kg。随机种子控制速度扰动，不引入随时间的随机行走噪声。

### 主体参数

| 参数 / 代码键 | 行人 | 车辆 | 说明 |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| 长度 `length` | 0.5 m | 4.5 m | 纵向矩形包络长度 |
| 宽度 `width` | 0.6 m | 1.8 m | 横向实体宽度，不包含安全裕度 |
| 期望/初始速度基值 | 1.3 m/s | 3.0 m/s | 同一种子在两种/三种模型中相同 |
| 速度扰动 `speed_jitter` | ±8% | ±8% | 均匀分布，初始速度等于扰动后的期望速度 |
| 纵向最大加速度 `max_accel` | 1.2 m/s² | 1.5 m/s² | 两侧使用同一限幅公式 |
| 最大制动 `max_brake` | 2.0 m/s² | 3.0 m/s² | 不允许瞬间停车 |
| 横向最大速度 `lateral_speed` | 0.7 m/s | 0 m/s；对照0.2 | 车辆默认固定横向位置 |
| 横向最大加速度 `lateral_accel` | 1.0 m/s² | 0.4 m/s² | 小幅侧移的机动限制 |
| 纵向松弛时间 `speed_tau` | 0.7 s | 0.7 s | 不等于感知反应时间 |
| 横向松弛时间 `lateral_tau` | 0.8 s | 0.8 s | 跟踪横向目标 |
| 交会速度上限 `encounter_speed` | 1.0 m/s | 1.0 m/s | 主规则按宽度再乘0.35～1系数；纯SFM不用该规则 |
| 意图方向 `direction` | −1 | +1 | 相向运动 |
| 避让侧 `side` | +1 | −1 | 默认固定选边，保持到错车完成 |
| 质量 `mass` | 70 kg | 1500 kg | 元数据；质量×0.1/1/10为无影响对照 |

### 场景与算法参数

| 参数 / 代码键 | 默认值 | 说明 |
|---|---:|---|
| 直路长度 | 60 m | 目标是主体尾部离开道路 |
| 基础宽度 | 3.2 m | 主宽度扫描5.0→2.4 m |
| 行人数 / 车数 | 3 / 2 | 不是连续到达过程，所有主体t=0已在路中 |
| 车辆起点s | 16、7 m | 车中心间隔9 m |
| 行人起点s | 44、46.5、49 m | 人中心间隔2.5 m |
| 初始相向中心距 `initial_gap` | 28 m | 主扫描12、20、28、34 m；不是表面净距 |
| 初始车道位置 | −W_min/2 + 0.1 + 1.8/2 | 根据最窄宽度提前靠边；居中另作对照 |
| 行人初始y / home_y | 同车辆初始y | 初始确实存在迎面冲突 |
| 缩窄道路宽度节点 | (0,5),(12,5),(18,W),(42,W),(48,5),(60,5) | 按s线性插值，静态空间缩窄 |
| 步长 `dt` | 0.05 s | 收敛对照0.1、0.05、0.025 s |
| 直路最长观察 `max_time` | 160 s | 未完成返回timeout |
| 交互前瞻 `horizon` | 6 s | 基于名义速度构造探测范围，最小8 m |
| 制动规划余量 `reaction_buffer` | 0.6 s | 本版不模拟信息延迟 |
| 人车侧向间距 `lateral_clearance` | 0.2 m | 扫描0、0.1、0.2、0.3、0.4 m |
| 道路边界裕度 `wall_clearance` | 每侧0.1 m | 与实体宽度分开 |
| 纵向静止间距 `longitudinal_clearance` | 0.6 m | 制动包络与互过判据使用 |
| 临界宽度速度比例 `squeeze_speed_fraction` | 0.35 | 透明的窄路慢行假设 |
| 舒适额外横向余量 `comfort_extra_gap` | 0.60 m | 达到该余量后交会速度比例为1 |
| 持续停滞时长 `stall_time` | 8 s | 所有未退出主体同时低速 |
| 低速阈值 `stop_speed` | 0.025 m/s | 使用二维速度模长 |
| 数值裕度容差 `clearance_tolerance` | 1e−5 m | 不代表额外实体空间 |
| 回放采样间隔 | 0.20 s；批量对照0.50 s | 只影响保存与显示，不影响0.05 s仿真 |

### 社会力参数

| 参数 | 默认值 | 探索范围 / 含义 |
|---|---:|---|
| A | 8 m/s² | {2,4,8,16}，主体排斥幅值 |
| B | 1.5 m | {0.4,0.8,1.5,2.5}，排斥衰减距离 |
| wall_A | 3 m/s² | 软墙排斥幅值 |
| wall_B | 0.2 m | 软墙作用距离 |
| anisotropy | 0.3 | 后方作用权重下限 |

A、B 探索在3.2 m、单人单车、种子100–102上完成；模型对照在少量多人多车、种子0–4上完成。只探索了有限参数，没有声称求得全局最优值。wall_A、wall_B、各向异性和行人个体差异尚未全面标定或搜索。

### 已执行的灵敏度设计

| 分组 | 取值 | 保持不变/注意 |
|---|---|---|
| 道路宽度 | 5,4.5,4,3.5,3.2,3,2.9,2.8,2.7,2.6,2.4 m | 默认几何下限2.4 m |
| 缩窄最小宽度 | 3.4,3.2,3,2.9,2.8,2.7,2.6,2.4 m | 两端5 m |
| 车辆期望/初始速度 | 1.5,3,4.5,6 m/s | 初始速度与期望速度一起变化 |
| 行人横向速度 | 0.15,0.3,0.7,1.0 m/s | 横向加速度保持1.0 m/s² |
| 侧向间距 | 0,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.4 m | 道路固定2.8 m |
| 初始相向中心距 | 12,20,28,34 m | 车起点不变，行人起点移动 |
| 行人宽度 | 0.45,0.6,0.75,0.9 m | 道路3.0 m |
| 车辆宽度 | 1.6,1.8,2.0,2.2 m | 道路3.0 m |
| 行人数 | 1,2,3,5 | 车辆保持2辆 |
| 数值步长 | 0.1,0.05,0.025 s | 道路2.9 m |
| 质量倍率 | 0.1,1,10 | 验证元数据不会偷偷改变行为 |
| 车道位置/横向能力 | 靠边固定、居中固定、居中可侧移 | 道路2.9 m |
| 宽度×车速 | {2.7,2.8,2.9,3.2,4.0}×{1.5,3,4.5} | 检查交互效应，不只做单因素 |
| 三模型直路比较 | 宽度5,4,3.2,2.9,2.8,2.6 m | 相同种子和初态 |
| 三模型缩窄比较 | 3.2,2.6 m | 相同缩窄节点 |

每个正式配置使用5个配对种子。重复次数有限，完成率附Wilson区间，不能把5/5解释为真实系统100%安全。

### 向心穿越参数

| 参数 | 默认值 | 对照 |
|---|---:|---|
| 初始半径 radius | 12 m | 8、12、16 m |
| 人车对数 pairs | 4，即4人4车交替排布 | 1、2、4、6 |
| 观察时长 duration | 120 s | 固定 |
| 侧移目标 passing_offset | 2.5 m | 固定；局部+n方向 |
| 终点容差 goal_tolerance | 0.35 m | 进入范围并停稳才完成 |
| 车横向速度上限 | 0 m/s | 0.2 m/s |
| 车初始/期望速度 | 3 m/s | 1.5 m/s |
| 圆周整体旋转 rotation | 0 rad | 旋转不变性测试0.371 rad |

向心实验复用主体尺寸、加减速、时间步及速度扰动；没有实体环道或墙力。8个配对设计共120次，详见CIRCLE.md（已纳入本文，相关文件也可重新运行生成）。

## 向心穿越实验补充

打开动态主页（已纳入本文，相关文件也可重新运行生成） · 独立回放（已纳入本文，相关文件也可重新运行生成） · 逐次指标（已纳入本文，相关文件也可重新运行生成） · 回到模型首页（已纳入本文，相关文件也可重新运行生成）

### 场景定义

人和车交替、等角度排列在圆周上，同时朝圆心方向出发，并以各自初始位置的对径点为终点。默认4人4车、初始半径12 m，观察120 s。初始圆周只是排布参考线，不是实体边界，也没有圆环道路。相邻类别交替不意味着同直径两端必定异类：4对主体时，同直径两端是同类。

主体中心进入终点0.35 m范围并停稳才计为到达。已经到达的主体保持原位置并继续作为实体障碍；没有到一个就删除一个，也没有把位置吸附到终点。终点停车同样受纵横向减速度约束。

### 现有算法如何用于多方向运动

原控制器只认识沿路纵向和横向。本次没有为人车分别设计控制器，而是在每个主体的固定初始朝向上建立局部坐标，再复用原来的期望运动、选边、速度限制和互惠制动公式。

```text
初始位置 p_i(0) = R (cos θ_i, sin θ_i)
目标位置 g_i = −p_i(0)
纵向单位向量 e_i = (−cos θ_i, −sin θ_i)
横向单位向量 n_i = (−e_iy, e_ix)
p_i(k+1) = p_i(k) + Δt [u_i(k+1)e_i + w_i(k+1)n_i]
```

计算主体i的控制时，将所有位置、速度投影到(e_i,n_i)。其他主体的旋转矩形投影为该坐标系中的包围矩形：投影长度/宽度由两轴上的支撑半径计算。其他主体朝向在e_i方向的分量大于1e−8时归为同向，其余归为相向；这是对原二方向规则的明确适配，不能称为已有完整多方向规划能力。

默认全体在各自局部坐标取同一侧(+n_i)，避免给不同全局朝向硬指定同一个世界坐标侧。交互目标侧移量为2.5 m；代码用宽度 `2×2.5 + 主体宽度 + 2×墙边裕度` 的虚拟路径带复用原横向目标和慢行公式。这里没有实体墙，SFM墙力关闭。无交互时恢复各自直径路径。

车辆横向速度默认0 m/s，另用0.2 m/s作对照；行人0.7 m/s。车速基值3 m/s，人速1.3 m/s；加速度、制动能力、松弛时间、0.2 m侧向间距和0.6 m纵向间距沿用原参数。随机性仍仅为速度±8%扰动，每配置种子0–4，三模型配对使用相同初态。

本次必要改动是坐标适配、对径终点停车和不同朝向的碰撞几何。主体初始朝向固定，侧移不引起车身转向，尚无转向角或自行车模型。投影包围矩形对斜向邻居较保守，可能增加停车；因此僵持既反映局部策略限制，也可能受到这项近似影响。

### 碰撞、完成与停滞

- 所有主体同步计算控制、同步推进，步长0.05 s。
- 物理检测使用固定朝向矩形的分离轴定理（SAT），计算整个步内的接触时刻，避免只查帧端漏掉交叉穿越。
- `min_pair_gap_m`在此场景为全步SAT间距的保守下界，不是原直路的精确欧氏间距。下界不足只能说没有证实裕度足够，不能据此声称发生接触；碰撞由独立连续SAT检测确定。
- 全体到达且停稳为completed；检测到实体接触为collision；所有未到达主体二维速度低于0.025 m/s持续8 s为deadlock_policy；120 s未结束为timeout。
- 本场景不使用窄路的2.4/2.8 m断面阈值，也不使用圈数、检测线通量或周期接缝指标。

### 已运行的对照

8组配置 × 3个模型 × 5个种子，共120次。除列出的变化外保持默认参数：

| 配置 | 变化 |
|---|---|
| baseline | 4人4车，R=12 m，车辆横向能力0 |
| mobile | 车辆横向速度上限改为0.2 m/s |
| one_pair | 1人1车，对向穿越复核 |
| two_pairs | 2人2车，十字汇聚 |
| dense | 6人6车 |
| radius_8 / radius_16 | 初始半径改为8 / 16 m |
| slow_vehicle | 车辆初始/期望速度基值改为1.5 m/s |

半径变化同时改变起点距离和总路程；车速变化同时改变初始与期望速度。尚未扫描侧移目标带宽、目标容差或所有朝向排列。

### 结果与原因边界

1. 单人单车中，统一规则和修正版均5/5完成，说明向心坐标、终点和回归路径能形成完整穿越；纯SFM在该组均僵持。
2. 默认4人4车中，三模型均5/5僵持，未发生碰撞；车辆允许0.2 m/s侧移后仍未全部通过。未通过不能写成“理论上没有空间”。
3. 2人2车中，纯SFM均发生碰撞；规则和修正版均等待。其余已测多主体设置也未全部到达。
4. 当前主体依据局部投影和独立停止空间限制行动，没有全局预约、倒退或交叉口交替放行。多个径向路径可能互相约束；不同朝向包围矩形投影又会放大占用。因此这是现有规则迁移后的失败结果，不是已证明真实场景必然无解。

没有为了获得成功曲线取消碰撞或把主体挪开，也没有把新增协调算法混入本次基线。后续若加入通行顺序或二维路径规划，应作为另一组明确标记的修正实验。

### 复现

从项目根目录运行：

```powershell
python -m pvsim run --config configs/ring.json --out results/custom_circle
python -m pvsim circle --seeds 5 --out results/circle_crossing
```

原沿环周期运动的150次对照及2个失败案例已从结果集合中删除。当前总计668次：360次原规则扫描、120次直路/缩窄三模型对照、48次SFM参数探索、11次失败修正、9次基础演示、120次向心穿越。数量包含演示和重复配置，不表示668个独立样本。

## 第6部分：恢复代码与运行方法

仿真核心、CSV/JSON导出和动画生成仅依赖Python 3.9+标准库；绘图另需matplotlib和numpy。源码按模型、空间拓扑、策略、场景、积分器、几何检测、实验和导出解耦。

如需重新运行，将下面的提取脚本另存为 `extract_code.py`，和本Markdown文件放在同一目录。它只提取第9部分带校验值的文件，不执行文档中的任意代码，并拒绝覆盖内容不同的已有文件。

```python
from pathlib import Path
import hashlib
import re
import sys

document = Path(sys.argv[1])
destination = Path(sys.argv[2]).resolve()
text = document.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
pattern = r'<!-- simfile path="([^"]+)" sha256="([a-f0-9]{64})" -->\n(`{3,})[^\n]*\n(.*?)\n\3\n<!-- /simfile -->'
blocks = re.findall(pattern, text, re.S)
if not blocks:
    raise SystemExit("No source blocks found")
prepared = []
for name, digest, fence, body in blocks:
    target = (destination / name).resolve()
    if destination not in target.parents:
        raise ValueError("Unsafe path: " + name)
    payload = (body + "\n").encode("utf-8")
    if hashlib.sha256(payload).hexdigest() != digest:
        raise ValueError("Source checksum mismatch: " + name)
    if target.exists() and target.read_bytes() != payload:
        raise FileExistsError("Refusing to replace different existing file: " + str(target))
    prepared.append((target, payload))
for target, payload in prepared:
    target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    target.write_bytes(payload)
print("Restored", len(prepared), "source/config/test files to", destination)
```

原项目目录与代码均保留，可直接在原项目中运行。若只有本文，可在PowerShell中执行以下命令，将代码恢复到新的 `recreated` 目录。

```powershell
python extract_code.py "week06_人车交互模型与实验.md" recreated
cd recreated
python -m unittest discover -s tests -v
python -m pvsim run --config configs/default.json --out results/custom
python -m pvsim run --config configs/ring.json --policy reciprocal --out results/custom_circle
python -m pvsim run --config configs/default.json --policy social_force --out results/custom_sfm
python -m pvsim run --config configs/default.json --policy sfm_guided --out results/custom_hybrid
```

完整重现668次正式结果和演示：

```powershell
python -m pvsim all --seeds 5 --out results
```

如需同时重新生成统计图：

```powershell
python -m pip install -r requirements-plot.txt
python -m pvsim all --seeds 5 --plots --out results
```

恢复的代码包含全部仿真、实验、回放、配置和28项测试；报告生成与网页渲染工具不收录，因为文档已经合并为本文件。原项目另提供 Markdown 与相互链接的 HTML，动态主页保留向心穿越回放。`kind`只用于统计/显示，不分派人车运动方程。自定义初始位置与尺寸见`configs/custom_layout.json`。

### pvsim/__init__.py

<!-- simfile path="pvsim/__init__.py" sha256="d9963fe1109f6879820baed2924f44812df656bd0d20c2f005420a819322f84b" -->
```python
"""Unified pedestrian–vehicle interaction research prototype (SI units)."""

from .domain import AgentSpec, AgentState, Road, Settings, Scenario
from .policy import ReciprocalPolicy
from .simulation import simulate

__all__ = ["AgentSpec", "AgentState", "Road", "Settings", "Scenario", "ReciprocalPolicy", "simulate"]
```
<!-- /simfile -->

### pvsim/__main__.py

<!-- simfile path="pvsim/__main__.py" sha256="1088d0fda46d544f2f325751ac08cb722170893fcd7c7a55f4bdb08bd0a6835e" -->
```python
import argparse
from pathlib import Path
from .scenarios import corridor, load_scenario
from .simulation import simulate
from .export import save_result, save_replay
from .experiments import run_sweep, make_plots
from .social_force import make_policy
from .benchmark import benchmark, sfm_parameter_sweep, failures
from .radial_experiments import run_radial


def demos(out):
    definitions = [
        ("wide", "5.0 m · 3 人 / 2 车", dict(width=5)),
        ("threshold", "2.8 m · 安全宽度临界值", dict(width=2.8)),
        ("clearance_wait", "2.6 m · 几何可放下 / 安全间距不足", dict(width=2.6)),
        ("physical_limit", "2.4 m · 几何下限 / 等待", dict(width=2.4)),
        ("bottleneck_pass", "5 → 3.2 → 5 m · 缩窄道路通过", dict(width=3.2, bottleneck=True)),
        ("bottleneck_wait", "5 → 2.6 → 5 m · 缩窄道路等待", dict(width=2.6, bottleneck=True)),
        ("center_fixed", "2.9 m · 车辆居中且不侧移", dict(width=2.9, centered_vehicle=True)),
        ("center_mobile", "2.9 m · 允许车辆小幅靠边", dict(width=2.9, centered_vehicle=True, vehicle_lateral_speed=.2)),
        ("late_conflict", "3.2 m · 近距离相遇 / 先停再绕", dict(width=3.2, initial_gap=12, lateral_speed=.15)),
    ]
    results = []
    for slug, label, params in definitions:
        result = simulate(corridor(**params))
        save_result(result, out / slug)
        results.append((label, result))
        print(f"{slug}: {result['metrics']['status']}", flush=True)
    save_replay(results, out / "replay.html")
    return results


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Unified pedestrian-vehicle interaction simulator")
    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
    for command in ("run", "demo", "sweep", "benchmark", "failures", "sfm-sweep", "circle", "all"):
        p = sub.add_parser(command)
        p.add_argument("--out", type=Path, default=Path("results"))
        if command == "run":
            p.add_argument("--config", type=Path, default=Path("configs/default.json"))
            p.add_argument("--policy", choices=("reciprocal", "social_force", "sfm_guided", "sfm_guided_ungated"), default="reciprocal")
        if command in ("sweep", "benchmark", "circle", "all"):
            p.add_argument("--seeds", type=int, default=5)
            p.add_argument("--plots", action="store_true", help="Requires matplotlib and numpy")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.command == "run":
        result = simulate(load_scenario(args.config), make_policy(args.policy))
        save_result(result, args.out)
        save_replay([(result["scenario"]["name"], result)], args.out / "replay.html")
        print(result["metrics"])
    if args.command in ("demo", "all"):
        demos(args.out / "demo" if args.command == "all" else args.out)
    if args.command in ("sweep", "all"):
        directory = args.out / "experiments" if args.command == "all" else args.out
        rows, summary = run_sweep(directory, args.seeds)
        if args.plots:
            make_plots(rows, summary, directory)
        print(f"Saved {len(rows)} runs to {directory}")
    if args.command in ("benchmark", "all"):
        directory = args.out / "benchmark" if args.command == "all" else args.out
        benchmark(directory, args.seeds, args.plots)
    if args.command in ("sfm-sweep", "all"):
        sfm_parameter_sweep(args.out / "sfm_parameters" if args.command == "all" else args.out)
    if args.command in ("failures", "all"):
        failures(args.out / "failures" if args.command == "all" else args.out)
    if args.command in ("circle", "all"):
        run_radial(args.out / "circle_crossing" if args.command == "all" else args.out, args.seeds)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

### pvsim/benchmark.py

<!-- simfile path="pvsim/benchmark.py" sha256="533aefaff44d486dca80f0832d763251ffa3f9c89fd57b0d6c849cd141b3e655" -->
```python
from collections import defaultdict, Counter
from dataclasses import replace
from pathlib import Path
import json
import statistics
from .scenarios import corridor
from .simulation import simulate
from .social_force import make_policy, SocialForceParameters, SocialForcePolicy
from .domain import Settings
from .export import save_result, save_replay
from .experiments import mean, write_csv, wilson


POLICIES = ("reciprocal", "social_force", "sfm_guided")


def benchmark_designs():
    for w in (5, 4, 3.2, 2.9, 2.8, 2.6):
        yield "straight", w, corridor, {"width": w}
    for w in (3.2, 2.6):
        yield "bottleneck", w, corridor, {"width": w, "bottleneck": True}


def benchmark(directory, seeds=5, plots=False):
    out = Path(directory)
    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    rows = []
    replay = []
    with (out / "runs.jsonl").open("w", encoding="utf-8") as log:
        for group, value, factory, params in benchmark_designs():
            for seed in range(seeds):
                scene = factory(seed=seed, settings=Settings(record_every=10), **params)
                for policy in POLICIES:
                    record = seed == 0 and group == "straight" and value in (5, 3.2, 2.8, 2.6)
                    result = simulate(scene, make_policy(policy), record=record)
                    m = result["metrics"]
                    row = {"group": group, "value": value, "seed": seed, "policy": policy,
                           "status": m["status"], "full_observation_success": m["status"] in ("completed", "sustained"),
                           "safety_margins_satisfied": m["safety_margins_satisfied"],
                           "completion_time_s": m["completion_time_s"], "observation_time_s": m["observation_time_s"],
                           "min_pair_gap_m": m["min_pair_gap_m"], "min_wall_gap_m": m["min_wall_gap_m"],
                           "collision_pairs": m["collision_pairs"], "boundary_violations": m["boundary_violations"],
                           "pair_passages": m["pair_passages"], "total_agent_waiting_s": m["total_agent_waiting_s"],
                           "effective_speed_planned_horizon_m_s": m["effective_speed_planned_horizon_m_s"],
                           "pair_passages_per_min_planned": m["pair_passages_per_min_planned"]}
                    rows.append(row)
                    if record:
                        slug = f"{group}_{value}_{policy}"
                        save_result(result, out / "trajectories" / slug)
                        label = f"直路 {value} m · {policy}"
                        replay.append((label, result))
                    log.write(json.dumps({"design": {"group": group, "value": value}, "result": dict(result, trace=[])}, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
            print(f"benchmark {group}={value}: {seeds} paired seeds x 3 policies", flush=True)
    groups = defaultdict(list)
    for row in rows:
        groups[(row["group"], row["value"], row["policy"])].append(row)
    summary = []
    for (group, value, policy), trials in groups.items():
        times = [r["completion_time_s"] for r in trials if r["completion_time_s"] is not None]
        successes = sum(r["full_observation_success"] for r in trials)
        lo, hi = wilson(successes, len(trials))
        summary.append({"group": group, "value": value, "policy": policy, "runs": len(trials),
                        "success_rate": successes / len(trials), "wilson_95_low": lo, "wilson_95_high": hi,
                        "safe_success_rate": sum(r["full_observation_success"] and r["safety_margins_satisfied"] for r in trials) / len(trials),
                        "completion_mean_s": mean(times), "completion_sd_s": statistics.stdev(times) if len(times)>1 else None,
                        "collision_runs": sum(r["collision_pairs"] > 0 for r in trials),
                        "boundary_violation_runs": sum(r["boundary_violations"] > 0 for r in trials),
                        "min_pair_gap_m": min(r["min_pair_gap_m"] for r in trials),
                        "min_wall_gap_m": min(r["min_wall_gap_m"] for r in trials),
                        "mean_pair_passages": mean([r["pair_passages"] for r in trials]),
                        "mean_effective_speed_m_s": mean([r["effective_speed_planned_horizon_m_s"] for r in trials]),
                        "mean_passages_per_min": mean([r["pair_passages_per_min_planned"] for r in trials]),
                        "status_counts": json.dumps(dict(Counter(r["status"] for r in trials)))})
    write_csv(out / "runs.csv", rows)
    write_csv(out / "summary.csv", summary)
    (out / "summary.json").write_text(json.dumps(summary, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    save_replay(replay, out / "replay.html")
    if plots:
        benchmark_plots(summary, out)
    return rows, summary


def sfm_parameter_sweep(directory):
    out = Path(directory)
    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    rows = []
    for A in (2, 4, 8, 16):
        for B in (.4, .8, 1.5, 2.5):
            for seed in (100, 101, 102):
                result = simulate(corridor(width=3.2, pedestrians=1, vehicles=1, seed=seed),
                                  SocialForcePolicy(SocialForceParameters(A=A, B=B)), record=False)
                m = result["metrics"]
                rows.append({"A_m_s2": A, "B_m": B, "seed": seed, "status": m["status"],
                             "completion_time_s": m["completion_time_s"], "min_pair_gap_m": m["min_pair_gap_m"],
                             "min_wall_gap_m": m["min_wall_gap_m"], "safe": m["safety_margins_satisfied"]})
    write_csv(out / "sfm_parameters.csv", rows)
    (out / "sfm_parameters.json").write_text(json.dumps(rows, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    return rows


def failures(directory):
    out = Path(directory)
    cases = [
        ("width_wait", "空间不足：2.6 m 等待", corridor(width=2.6), "reciprocal", None),
        ("width_repair", "扩大宽度：2.8 m 通过", corridor(width=2.8), "reciprocal", None),
        ("fixed_center", "车居中：2.9 m 阻塞", corridor(width=2.9, centered_vehicle=True), "reciprocal", None),
        ("move_vehicle", "允许车侧移：2.9 m 通过", corridor(width=2.9, centered_vehicle=True, vehicle_lateral_speed=.2), "reciprocal", None),
        ("excess_speed", "制动距离不足：初速 12 m/s", corridor(width=3.2, initial_gap=5, vehicle_speed=12, pedestrians=1, vehicles=1, speed_jitter=0), "reciprocal", None),
        ("speed_repair", "只降低初始/期望车速：1.5 m/s", corridor(width=3.2, initial_gap=5, vehicle_speed=1.5, pedestrians=1, vehicles=1, speed_jitter=0), "reciprocal", None),
        ("sfm_short", "SFM：短作用距离导致风险", corridor(width=5), "social_force", SocialForceParameters(A=4, B=.8)),
        ("sfm_tuned_wide", "SFM：扩大作用范围，5 m 通过", corridor(width=5), "social_force", None),
        ("sfm_narrow", "SFM：3.2 m 窄路软边界不足", corridor(width=3.2), "social_force", None),
        ("sfm_partial", "部分修正：选边与制动，仍可能僵持", corridor(width=3.2), "sfm_guided_ungated", None),
        ("sfm_full", "完整修正：解除已消除的冲突力", corridor(width=3.2), "sfm_guided", None),
    ]
    replay, rows = [], []
    for slug, label, scene, policy, params in cases:
        result = simulate(scene, make_policy(policy, params))
        save_result(result, out / slug)
        replay.append((label, result))
        rows.append({"case": slug, "label": label, "policy": policy, **{k: result["metrics"][k] for k in
                    ("status", "completion_time_s", "collision_pairs", "boundary_violations", "pair_passages",
                     "min_pair_gap_m", "min_wall_gap_m", "total_agent_waiting_s", "safety_margins_satisfied")}})
    write_csv(out / "comparison.csv", rows)
    (out / "comparison.json").write_text(json.dumps(rows, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    save_replay(replay, out / "replay.html")
    return rows


def benchmark_plots(summary, out):
    import matplotlib
    matplotlib.use("Agg")
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    colors = {"reciprocal": "#197b92", "social_force": "#c46e24", "sfm_guided": "#8066ad"}
    names = {"reciprocal": "Shared rules", "social_force": "SFM baseline", "sfm_guided": "SFM + rules + release"}
    fig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(12, 4.4), constrained_layout=True)
    for policy in POLICIES:
        straight = sorted([r for r in summary if r["group"] == "straight" and r["policy"] == policy], key=lambda r:r["value"])
        kw = dict(color=colors[policy], marker="o", label=names[policy])
        axes[0].plot([r["value"] for r in straight], [r["success_rate"] for r in straight], **kw)
        valid = [r for r in straight if r["completion_mean_s"] is not None]
        axes[1].plot([r["value"] for r in valid], [r["completion_mean_s"] for r in valid], **kw)
    axes[0].set(title="A  Straight-road completion", ylabel="Completed fraction", ylim=(-.05,1.08))
    axes[1].set(title="B  Conditional completion time", ylabel="Mean time among completed runs (s)")
    for a in axes.flat:
        a.set_xlabel("Road width (m)")
        a.grid(axis="y", alpha=.2)
        a.axvline(2.8, ls="--", lw=1, color="#667c8b")
        a.spines['top'].set_visible(False)
        a.spines['right'].set_visible(False)
    axes[0].legend(frameon=False, fontsize=8, loc="lower right")
    fig.suptitle("Matched initial states and limits | Uncalibrated social-force baseline", fontsize=14)
    fig.savefig(Path(out) / "model_comparison.png", dpi=180)
    fig.savefig(Path(out) / "model_comparison.svg")
    plt.close(fig)
```

### pvsim/diagnostics.py

<!-- simfile path="pvsim/diagnostics.py" sha256="62e1b40a25fea614f2542c3c08f83e036e7f0378dbc4e9a6746a979311cbfb8b" -->
```python
"""Necessary cross-section tests; deliberately not a global reachability proof."""
from itertools import combinations


def feasibility(scenario):
    c, road = scenario.settings, scenario.road
    pairs = [(a, b) for a, b in combinations(scenario.agents, 2)
             if a.spec.direction != b.spec.direction]
    physical = max((a.spec.width + b.spec.width for a, b in pairs), default=0.0)
    safe = physical + 2 * c.wall_clearance + c.lateral_clearance
    pose = True
    for a, b in pairs:
        for fixed, mobile in ((a, b), (b, a)):
            if fixed.spec.lateral_speed == 0:
                w = road.min_width()
                # Mobile's committed passing side is part of the controller's constraints.
                room = w / 2 - mobile.spec.side * fixed.y - fixed.spec.width / 2 - c.wall_clearance
                if room + c.clearance_tolerance < mobile.spec.width + c.lateral_clearance:
                    pose = False
    w = road.min_width()
    return {"minimum_road_width_m": w, "physical_width_threshold_m": physical,
            "safe_width_threshold_m": safe,
            "physical_cross_section_fits": w + c.clearance_tolerance >= physical,
            "safe_cross_section_fits": w + c.clearance_tolerance >= safe,
            "fixed_pose_cross_section_fits": pose,
            "scope": "Necessary cross-section conditions at the narrowest section; no global path or liveness guarantee."}


def classify_stall(scenario, agents):
    facts = feasibility(scenario)
    if not facts["physical_cross_section_fits"]:
        return "waiting_geometry"
    if not facts["safe_cross_section_fits"]:
        return "waiting_clearance"
    if not facts["fixed_pose_cross_section_fits"]:
        return "waiting_alignment"
    return "deadlock_policy"
```

### pvsim/domain.py

<!-- simfile path="pvsim/domain.py" sha256="289545c39f2aad5da12280bf1d86a9e31ba2f2da966fcb01f0dcbb2a37514dd1" -->
```python
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import List, Tuple
import math


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AgentSpec:
    """Kind is metadata only: the policy never branches on pedestrian/vehicle."""
    id: str
    kind: str
    length: float
    width: float
    desired_speed: float
    max_accel: float
    max_brake: float
    lateral_speed: float
    lateral_accel: float
    direction: int
    side: int
    home_y: float
    mass: float = 1.0  # Metadata, not a social priority or an interaction force.
    speed_tau: float = 0.7
    lateral_tau: float = 0.8
    encounter_speed: float = 1.0

    def __post_init__(self):
        for name in ("length", "width", "desired_speed", "max_accel", "max_brake",
                     "lateral_accel", "mass", "speed_tau", "lateral_tau", "encounter_speed"):
            value = getattr(self, name)
            if not math.isfinite(value) or value <= 0:
                raise ValueError(f"{self.id}: {name} must be positive and finite")
        if not math.isfinite(self.lateral_speed) or self.lateral_speed < 0:
            raise ValueError("lateral_speed must be finite and nonnegative")
        if self.direction not in (-1, 1) or self.side not in (-1, 1):
            raise ValueError("direction and side must be -1 or +1")


@dataclass
class AgentState:
    spec: AgentSpec
    x: float
    y: float
    speed: float  # Nonnegative speed along direction.
    vy: float = 0.0
    mode: str = "FREE"
    committed: bool = False
    done: bool = False
    exit_time: float = None
    waiting_time: float = 0.0
    path_length: float = 0.0
    peak_decel: float = 0.0

    @property
    def vx(self):
        return self.spec.direction * self.speed


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Road:
    """Symmetric, piecewise-linear road width w(x), extended at both ends."""
    length: float
    profile: Tuple[Tuple[float, float], ...]
    periodic: bool = False
    physical_walls: bool = True

    def __post_init__(self):
        if self.length <= 0 or len(self.profile) < 2:
            raise ValueError("Road needs positive length and >=2 profile points")
        if any(not math.isfinite(x) or not math.isfinite(w) or w <= 0 for x, w in self.profile):
            raise ValueError("Invalid width profile")
        if any(b[0] <= a[0] for a, b in zip(self.profile, self.profile[1:])):
            raise ValueError("Profile x coordinates must strictly increase")
        if self.profile[0][0] != 0 or self.profile[-1][0] != self.length:
            raise ValueError("Profile must cover [0, length]")
        if self.periodic and len(set(w for _, w in self.profile)) != 1:
            raise ValueError("The periodic-strip baseline currently requires constant width")

    def width_at(self, x):
        if x <= self.profile[0][0]:
            return self.profile[0][1]
        for (x0, w0), (x1, w1) in zip(self.profile, self.profile[1:]):
            if x <= x1:
                return w0 + (w1 - w0) * (x - x0) / (x1 - x0)
        return self.profile[-1][1]

    def min_width(self, x0=None, x1=None):
        if x0 is None:
            return min(w for _, w in self.profile)
        lo, hi = sorted((x0, x1))
        return min([self.width_at(lo), self.width_at(hi)] +
                   [w for x, w in self.profile if lo <= x <= hi])


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Settings:
    dt: float = 0.05
    max_time: float = 160.0
    horizon: float = 6.0
    reaction_buffer: float = 0.6  # Planning reserve, NOT a delayed-observation simulator.
    lateral_clearance: float = 0.20
    wall_clearance: float = 0.10
    longitudinal_clearance: float = 0.60
    stall_time: float = 8.0
    stop_speed: float = 0.025
    record_every: int = 4
    clearance_tolerance: float = 1e-5
    squeeze_speed_fraction: float = 0.35
    comfort_extra_gap: float = 0.60

    def __post_init__(self):
        for name in ("dt", "max_time", "horizon", "stall_time", "stop_speed"):
            if not math.isfinite(getattr(self, name)) or getattr(self, name) <= 0:
                raise ValueError(f"{name} must be positive and finite")
        for name in ("reaction_buffer", "lateral_clearance", "wall_clearance", "longitudinal_clearance"):
            if not math.isfinite(getattr(self, name)) or getattr(self, name) < 0:
                raise ValueError(f"{name} must be nonnegative and finite")
        if self.record_every < 1:
            raise ValueError("record_every must be >=1")
        if not 0 < self.squeeze_speed_fraction <= 1 or self.comfort_extra_gap <= 0:
            raise ValueError("Invalid squeeze-speed parameters")


@dataclass
class Scenario:
    name: str
    road: Road
    agents: List[AgentState]
    settings: Settings = field(default_factory=Settings)
    seed: int = 0
    metadata: dict = field(default_factory=dict)
```

### pvsim/experiments.py

<!-- simfile path="pvsim/experiments.py" sha256="0a6ca3eab1743c6b0ffce4fbcbf44f3d831b6fc223e15a91d96a81a7f73047de" -->
```python
"""Paired-seed sensitivity runs; uncompleted times are censored (None), not zero."""
from dataclasses import replace
from collections import defaultdict, Counter
from pathlib import Path
import csv
import json
import math
import statistics
from .domain import Settings
from .scenarios import corridor
from .simulation import simulate


def designs():
    for width in (5, 4.5, 4, 3.5, 3.2, 3, 2.9, 2.8, 2.7, 2.6, 2.4):
        yield "width", width, {"width": width}, {}
    for width in (3.4, 3.2, 3, 2.9, 2.8, 2.7, 2.6, 2.4):
        yield "bottleneck", width, {"width": width, "bottleneck": True}, {}
    for value in (1.5, 3, 4.5, 6):
        yield "vehicle_speed", value, {"vehicle_speed": value}, {}
    for value in (.15, .3, .7, 1.0):
        yield "pedestrian_lateral_speed", value, {"lateral_speed": value}, {}
    for value in (0, .1, .2, .3, .4):
        yield "lateral_clearance", value, {"width": 2.8}, {"lateral_clearance": value}
    for value in (12, 20, 28, 34):
        yield "initial_gap", value, {"initial_gap": value}, {}
    for value in (.45, .6, .75, .9):
        yield "pedestrian_width", value, {"width": 3.0, "pedestrian_width": value}, {}
    for value in (1.6, 1.8, 2.0, 2.2):
        yield "vehicle_width", value, {"width": 3.0, "vehicle_width": value}, {}
    for value in (1, 2, 3, 5):
        yield "pedestrian_count", value, {"pedestrians": value}, {}
    for value in (.1, .05, .025):
        yield "dt", value, {"width": 2.9}, {"dt": value}
    for value in (.1, 1, 10):
        yield "mass_scale", value, {}, {}
        # This is a negative-control parameter: mass only changes metadata.
    yield "alignment", "edge_fixed", {"width": 2.9}, {}
    yield "alignment", "center_fixed", {"width": 2.9, "centered_vehicle": True}, {}
    yield "alignment", "center_mobile", {"width": 2.9, "centered_vehicle": True, "vehicle_lateral_speed": .2}, {}
    for width in (2.7, 2.8, 2.9, 3.2, 4.0):
        for speed in (1.5, 3, 4.5):
            yield "width_x_speed", f"{width}:{speed}", {"width": width, "vehicle_speed": speed}, {}


def mean(values):
    values = [x for x in values if x is not None]
    return statistics.mean(values) if values else None


def wilson(k, n):
    z = 1.95996398454
    center = (k / n + z * z / (2 * n)) / (1 + z * z / n)
    half = z * math.sqrt(k / n * (1 - k / n) / n + z * z / (4 * n * n)) / (1 + z * z / n)
    return max(0.0, center - half), min(1.0, center + half)


def write_csv(path, rows):
    if not rows:
        return
    with Path(path).open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8-sig") as f:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(f, list(rows[0]))
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(rows)


def run_sweep(directory, seeds=5):
    if seeds < 1:
        raise ValueError("seeds must be positive")
    out = Path(directory)
    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    rows = []
    with (out / "runs.jsonl").open("w", encoding="utf-8") as log:
        for axis, value, params, options in designs():
            if axis == "mass_scale":
                params = dict(params, mass_scale=value)
            for seed in range(seeds):
                config = dict(width=3.2, seed=seed)
                config.update(params)
                scenario = corridor(settings=replace(Settings(), **options), **config)
                result = simulate(scenario, record=False)
                m = result["metrics"]
                row = {"axis": axis, "value": value, "seed": seed, "width_m": scenario.road.min_width(),
                       "vehicle_desired_speed_base_m_s": config.get("vehicle_speed", 3.0),
                       "status": m["status"], "completed": int(m["status"] == "completed"),
                       "completion_time_s": m["completion_time_s"], "observation_time_s": m["observation_time_s"],
                       "pedestrians_passed": m["pedestrians_passed"], "vehicles_passed": m["vehicles_passed"],
                       "collision_pairs": m["collision_pairs"], "boundary_violations": m["boundary_violations"],
                       "min_pair_gap_m": m["min_pair_gap_m"], "min_wall_gap_m": m["min_wall_gap_m"],
                       "total_agent_waiting_s": m["total_agent_waiting_s"],
                       "mean_path_length_m": mean([a["path_length_m"] for a in m["agents"]]),
                       "peak_decel_m_s2": max(a["peak_decel_m_s2"] for a in m["agents"]),
                       "speed_ceiling_unreachable_agent_steps": m["speed_ceiling_unreachable_agent_steps"]}
                rows.append(row)
                log.write(json.dumps({"design": {"axis": axis, "value": value}, "result": result}, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
            print(f"{axis}={value}: {seeds} runs", flush=True)
    grouped = defaultdict(list)
    for row in rows:
        grouped[(row["axis"], row["value"])].append(row)
    summary = []
    for (axis, value), trials in grouped.items():
        successful = [x["completion_time_s"] for x in trials if x["completed"]]
        lo, hi = wilson(len(successful), len(trials))
        summary.append({"axis": axis, "value": value, "runs": len(trials),
                        "success_rate": len(successful) / len(trials),
                        "wilson_95_low": lo, "wilson_95_high": hi,
                        "completion_mean_s": mean(successful),
                        "completion_sd_s": statistics.stdev(successful) if len(successful) > 1 else None,
                        "completion_p95_s": sorted(successful)[math.ceil(.95 * len(successful)) - 1] if successful else None,
                        "observation_mean_s": mean([x["observation_time_s"] for x in trials]),
                        "min_pair_gap_m": min(x["min_pair_gap_m"] for x in trials),
                        "collision_run_count": sum(x["collision_pairs"] > 0 for x in trials),
                        "wall_violation_run_count": sum(x["boundary_violations"] > 0 for x in trials),
                        "mean_total_agent_waiting_s": mean([x["total_agent_waiting_s"] for x in trials]),
                        "status_counts": json.dumps(dict(Counter(x["status"] for x in trials)), ensure_ascii=False)})
    write_csv(out / "runs.csv", rows)
    write_csv(out / "summary.csv", summary)
    (out / "summary.json").write_text(json.dumps(summary, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    return rows, summary


def make_plots(rows, summary, directory):
    import matplotlib
    matplotlib.use("Agg")
    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
    import numpy as np
    plt.rcParams.update({"font.family": "DejaVu Sans", "font.size": 10,
                         "axes.spines.top": False, "axes.spines.right": False,
                         "savefig.facecolor": "white", "figure.facecolor": "white"})
    widths = sorted([r for r in summary if r["axis"] == "width"], key=lambda r: r["value"])
    x = [r["value"] for r in widths]
    fig, axes = plt.subplots(2, 2, figsize=(12, 8.4), constrained_layout=True)
    a = axes[0, 0]
    rate = [r["success_rate"] for r in widths]
    a.errorbar(x, rate, yerr=[[max(0, r["success_rate"]-r["wilson_95_low"]) for r in widths],
                            [max(0, r["wilson_95_high"]-r["success_rate"]) for r in widths]], fmt="o-", capsize=3, color="#197b92")
    a.set(title="A  Completion probability (95% Wilson intervals)", xlabel="Road width (m)", ylabel="Fraction of runs completed", ylim=(-.05, 1.1))
    a = axes[0, 1]
    completed = [r for r in widths if r["completion_mean_s"] is not None]
    a.errorbar([r["value"] for r in completed], [r["completion_mean_s"] for r in completed],
               yerr=[r["completion_sd_s"] or 0 for r in completed], fmt="o-", capsize=3, color="#197b92")
    a.set(title="B  Completion time among completed runs (mean +/- SD)", xlabel="Road width (m)", ylabel="Time until all trailing edges exit (s)")
    a = axes[1, 0]
    a.plot(x, [r["min_pair_gap_m"] for r in widths], "o-", color="#c46e24")
    a.axhline(.2, color="#667c8b", lw=1, ls=":", label="Lateral clearance = 0.2 m")
    a.set(title="C  Minimum physical pair gap across runs", xlabel="Road width (m)", ylabel="Gap (m)")
    a.legend(frameon=False, fontsize=8, loc="upper left")
    for a in (axes[0, 0], axes[0, 1], axes[1, 0]):
        a.axvspan(2.4, 2.8, color="#bc4157", alpha=.07)
        a.axvline(2.8, color="#667c8b", lw=1, ls="--")
        a.set_xlim(2.32, 5.08)
        a.grid(axis="y", alpha=.15)
    matrix = [r for r in summary if r["axis"] == "width_x_speed"]
    ws, vs = (2.7, 2.8, 2.9, 3.2, 4.0), (1.5, 3, 4.5)
    z = np.full((len(vs), len(ws)), np.nan)
    for r in matrix:
        w, v = map(float, str(r["value"]).split(":"))
        if r["completion_mean_s"] is not None:
            z[vs.index(v), ws.index(w)] = r["completion_mean_s"]
    a = axes[1, 1]
    cmap = plt.get_cmap("YlGnBu").copy()
    cmap.set_bad("#eef1f4")
    image = a.imshow(z, aspect="auto", cmap=cmap)
    a.set_xticks(range(len(ws)), [str(w) for w in ws])
    a.set_yticks(range(len(vs)), [str(v) for v in vs])
    a.set(title="D  Width x initial/desired vehicle speed", xlabel="Road width (m)", ylabel="Vehicle nominal speed (m/s)")
    for iy in range(len(vs)):
        for ix in range(len(ws)):
            val = z[iy, ix]
            a.text(ix, iy, "wait" if np.isnan(val) else f"{val:.1f}", ha="center", va="center",
                   color="#3b4f5b" if np.isnan(val) or val < np.nanmedian(z) else "white", fontsize=9)
    fig.colorbar(image, ax=a, label="Mean completion time (s)", fraction=.045)
    fig.suptitle("Unified pedestrian-vehicle model | 3 pedestrians, 2 vehicles", fontsize=15)
    out = Path(directory)
    fig.savefig(out / "sensitivity.png", dpi=180)
    fig.savefig(out / "sensitivity.svg")
    plt.close(fig)
```

### pvsim/export.py

```python
import csv
import json
from pathlib import Path


def save_result(result, directory):
    directory = Path(directory)
    directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (directory / "result.json").write_text(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    with (directory / "trajectory.csv").open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8-sig") as f:
        fields = ["time_s", "id", "x_m", "y_m", "speed_m_s", "vy_m_s", "mode", "done"]
        writer = csv.writer(f)
        writer.writerow(fields)
        for frame in result["trace"]:
            for a in frame["agents"]:
                writer.writerow([frame["time"], a["id"], a["x"], a["y"], a["speed"], a["vy"], a["mode"], a["done"]])


def save_replay(results, path):
    template = (Path(__file__).parent / "replay.html").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    # The replay is self-contained: no server, CDN or network needed.
    compact = []
    for label, result in results:
        compact.append({"label": label, "scenario": result["scenario"], "metrics": result["metrics"],
                        "feasibility": result.get("feasibility", {}), "trace": result["trace"], "policy": result.get("policy", {})})
    payload = json.dumps(compact, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":")).replace("<", "\\u003c")
    target = Path(path)
    target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    target.write_text(template.replace("__SIMULATION_DATA__", payload), encoding="utf-8")


def save_homepage(replay_path, root):
    """The homepage itself contains the circle replay; no server is required."""
    page = Path(replay_path).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    navigation = ('<nav style="display:flex;gap:18px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:20px">'
                  '<strong>圆环向心穿越</strong><a href="docs/index.html">文档导航</a>'
                  '<a href="README.html">模型与规则</a><a href="docs/REPORT.html">实验报告</a>'
                  '<a href="docs/CIRCLE.html">向心实验结果</a>'
                  '<a href="results/demo/replay.html">直路与缩窄</a>'
                  '<a href="results/benchmark/replay.html">直路模型对比</a>'
                  '<a href="results/failures/replay.html">失败与修正</a></nav>')
    page = page.replace('<body><main>', '<body><main>'+navigation, 1)
    page = page.replace('<title>窄路人车交互 · 仿真回放</title>', '<title>人车交互实验主页 · 圆环向心穿越</title>')
    (Path(root)/'index.html').write_text(page, encoding='utf-8')
```

### pvsim/geometry.py

<!-- simfile path="pvsim/geometry.py" sha256="ae0c29abc555287e481fd657cd88000d1eef93783b0e86ab56f9a587d259cc4d" -->
```python
"""Physical rectangle envelopes and continuous checks for linear timestep paths."""
import math


def separation(a, b):
    dx = max(abs(a.x - b.x) - (a.spec.length + b.spec.length) / 2, 0.0)
    dy = max(abs(a.y - b.y) - (a.spec.width + b.spec.width) / 2, 0.0)
    return math.hypot(dx, dy)


def swept_contact(a0, a1, b0, b1):
    """First contact fraction in [0,1], or None; touching counts as contact."""
    enter, leave = 0.0, 1.0
    for c0, c1, radius in (
        (a0.x - b0.x, a1.x - b1.x, (a0.spec.length + b0.spec.length) / 2),
        (a0.y - b0.y, a1.y - b1.y, (a0.spec.width + b0.spec.width) / 2),
    ):
        delta = c1 - c0
        if abs(delta) < 1e-14:
            if abs(c0) > radius:
                return None
        else:
            t0, t1 = sorted(((-radius - c0) / delta, (radius - c0) / delta))
            enter, leave = max(enter, t0), min(leave, t1)
            if enter > leave:
                return None
    return enter if enter <= 1 and leave >= 0 else None


def swept_separation(a0, a1, b0, b1):
    """Exact minimum Euclidean rectangle separation on a linear segment."""
    bases = [a0.x - b0.x, a0.y - b0.y]
    changes = [(a1.x - b1.x) - bases[0], (a1.y - b1.y) - bases[1]]
    radii = [(a0.spec.length + b0.spec.length) / 2, (a0.spec.width + b0.spec.width) / 2]
    cuts = {0.0, 1.0}
    for z, d, r in zip(bases, changes, radii):
        if abs(d) > 1e-14:
            for v in (-r, 0.0, r):
                t = (v - z) / d
                if 0 < t < 1:
                    cuts.add(t)
    cuts = sorted(cuts)
    def distance2(t):
        return sum(max(abs(z + d * t) - r, 0.0) ** 2 for z, d, r in zip(bases, changes, radii))
    best = min(distance2(t) for t in cuts)
    for lo, hi in zip(cuts, cuts[1:]):
        mid = (lo + hi) / 2
        aa, ab = 0.0, 0.0
        for z, d, r in zip(bases, changes, radii):
            v = z + d * mid
            if abs(v) > r:
                sign = 1.0 if v > 0 else -1.0
                intercept, slope = sign * z - r, sign * d
                aa += slope * slope
                ab += intercept * slope
        if aa > 0:
            t = max(lo, min(hi, -ab / aa))
            best = min(best, distance2(t))
    return math.sqrt(best)


def wall_gap(road, a):
    width = road.min_width(a.x - a.spec.length / 2, a.x + a.spec.length / 2)
    return width / 2 - abs(a.y) - a.spec.width / 2


def swept_wall_gap(road, a0, a1):
    """Exact minimum for piecewise-linear symmetric walls and linear motion."""
    fractions = {0.0, 1.0}
    dx = a1.x - a0.x
    if abs(dx) > 1e-14:
        for knot, _ in road.profile:
            for offset in (-a0.spec.length / 2, a0.spec.length / 2):
                t = (knot - a0.x - offset) / dx
                if 0 < t < 1:
                    fractions.add(t)
    minimum = float("inf")
    for t in fractions:
        x = a0.x + dx * t
        y = a0.y + (a1.y - a0.y) * t
        width = road.min_width(x - a0.spec.length / 2, x + a0.spec.length / 2)
        minimum = min(minimum, width / 2 - abs(y) - a0.spec.width / 2)
    return minimum
```

### pvsim/policy.py

<!-- simfile path="pvsim/policy.py" sha256="991fe5db982a78d8f766bbdcc0952da0c2fcf1d72172f15fc93b269f3497914d" -->
```python
"""One policy for all agents: desire relaxation + reciprocal braking envelopes.

This is a transparent baseline, not ORCA, a calibrated SFM, or a safety proof.
All controls are computed from the same pre-step snapshot.
"""
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Protocol, Sequence
import math
from .domain import AgentState, Road, Settings
from .topology import neighbor_images


def clip(value, low, high):
    return max(low, min(high, value))


def safe_speed(distance, brake, reserve):
    return max(0.0, math.sqrt((brake * reserve) ** 2 + 2 * brake * max(distance, 0.0)) - brake * reserve)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Control:
    speed: float
    vy: float
    mode: str
    committed: bool
    braking_infeasible: bool = False


class Policy(Protocol):
    def control(self, agent: AgentState, agents: Sequence[AgentState], road: Road, settings: Settings) -> Control:
        ...


class ReciprocalPolicy:
    name = "reciprocal"

    def control(self, agent, agents, road, settings):
        p, c = agent.spec, settings
        active = neighbor_images(agent, agents, road)
        encounters = []
        for b in active:
            if b.spec.direction == p.direction:
                continue
            front = p.direction * (b.x - agent.x)
            extent = (p.length + b.spec.length) / 2
            # Nominal speeds avoid losing sight of the interaction after braking.
            detection = max(8.0, (p.desired_speed + b.spec.desired_speed) * c.horizon)
            if -extent - c.longitudinal_clearance < front < detection + extent:
                encounters.append(b)

        committed = bool(encounters)
        # Look ahead to narrow walls using map geometry; no width oracle forces a stop.
        look = max(4.0, p.desired_speed * c.horizon)
        forward_x = agent.x + p.direction * look
        narrow = road.min_width(min(agent.x, forward_x) - p.length / 2,
                                max(agent.x, forward_x) + p.length / 2)
        limit = max(0.0, narrow / 2 - p.width / 2 - c.wall_clearance)
        if p.lateral_speed > 0:
            target_y = p.side * limit if committed else clip(p.home_y, -limit, limit)
        else:
            target_y = agent.y  # Exactly zero lateral mobility, same equation otherwise.
        error = target_y - agent.y
        desired_vy = clip(error / p.lateral_tau, -p.lateral_speed, p.lateral_speed)
        bound = safe_speed(abs(error), p.lateral_accel, c.dt)
        desired_vy = clip(desired_vy, -bound, bound)
        vy = agent.vy + clip(desired_vy - agent.vy, -p.lateral_accel * c.dt, p.lateral_accel * c.dt)

        target = p.desired_speed
        if encounters:
            spare = min(narrow - p.width - b.spec.width - 2 * c.wall_clearance - c.lateral_clearance
                        for b in encounters)
            factor = c.squeeze_speed_fraction + (1 - c.squeeze_speed_fraction) * clip(spare / c.comfort_extra_gap, 0.0, 1.0)
            target = min(target, p.encounter_speed * factor)
        # One speed budget for the velocity vector, including sideways motion.
        target = min(target, math.sqrt(max(p.desired_speed ** 2 - vy ** 2, 0.0)))
        speed = agent.speed + clip((target - agent.speed) / p.speed_tau,
                                   -p.max_brake, p.max_accel) * c.dt
        ceiling = p.desired_speed
        reserve = c.reaction_buffer + c.dt
        obstructed = False
        for b in active:
            front = p.direction * (b.x - agent.x)
            extent = (p.length + b.spec.length) / 2
            lat_extent = (p.width + b.spec.width) / 2
            lat_gap = abs(agent.y - b.y) - lat_extent
            # Include one-step lateral closure; never release solely on future intent.
            next_gap = abs((agent.y + vy * c.dt) - (b.y + b.vy * c.dt)) - lat_extent
            separated = min(lat_gap, next_gap) >= c.lateral_clearance - c.clearance_tolerance
            if separated:
                continue
            if b.spec.direction != p.direction:
                if front <= -extent - c.longitudinal_clearance:
                    continue
                gap = front - extent - c.longitudinal_clearance
                # Equal stopping-space allocation; each side responds independently.
                candidate = safe_speed(0.5 * gap, p.max_brake, reserve)
            else:
                if front <= 0:
                    continue
                gap = front - extent - c.longitudinal_clearance
                # Stationary-leader bound is conservative, avoids a hidden leader model.
                candidate = safe_speed(gap, p.max_brake, reserve)
            ceiling = min(ceiling, candidate)
            obstructed = obstructed or candidate < target

        infeasible = ceiling < agent.speed - p.max_brake * c.dt - 1e-9
        speed = clip(min(speed, ceiling), max(0.0, agent.speed - p.max_brake * c.dt),
                     agent.speed + p.max_accel * c.dt)
        if speed < c.stop_speed and abs(vy) < c.stop_speed:
            mode = "WAIT"
        elif speed < agent.speed - 1e-6:
            mode = "BRAKE"
        elif committed and abs(error) > 0.03 and p.lateral_speed > 0:
            mode = "SIDESTEP"
        elif committed:
            mode = "PASS"
        elif abs(error) > 0.03:
            mode = "RECOVER"
        elif obstructed:
            mode = "FOLLOW"
        else:
            mode = "FREE"
        return Control(speed, vy, mode, committed, infeasible)
```

### pvsim/radial.py

<!-- simfile path="pvsim/radial.py" sha256="c2964c51b4eca16663fb2876a72a03410edba76772029555f4d0c3965a536cc1" -->
```python
"""Circle crossing: alternating vehicles/pedestrians moving to antipodal goals.

The corridor controllers are reused in each agent's fixed heading frame.
Other oriented footprints are conservatively projected into that frame.
No circular wall, signal, priority or collision projection is introduced.
"""
from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict, replace
from itertools import combinations
import copy
import math
import random
from .domain import AgentSpec, AgentState, Settings, Road
from .policy import ReciprocalPolicy, Control, safe_speed, clip
from .radial_geometry import basis, dot, support, projected_gap, swept_obb_contact, swept_gap_bound


@dataclass
class RadialScenario:
    agents: list
    headings: dict
    goals: dict
    settings: Settings
    radius: float
    passing_offset: float
    goal_tolerance: float
    seed: int
    metadata: dict
    name: str = 'circle_crossing'


def circle_crossing(radius=12.0, pairs=4, vehicle_lateral_speed=0.0, vehicle_speed=3.0,
                    pedestrian_speed=1.3, passing_offset=2.5, goal_tolerance=0.35,
                    duration=120.0, seed=0, speed_jitter=0.08, rotation=0.0, settings=None):
    if radius <= 0 or not isinstance(pairs, int) or pairs < 1 or passing_offset <= 0 or goal_tolerance <= 0:
        raise ValueError('Invalid circle-crossing geometry')
    c = replace(settings or Settings(), max_time=duration)
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    agents, headings, goals = [], {}, {}
    for i in range(2 * pairs):
        theta = rotation + math.pi * i / pairs
        vehicle = i % 2 == 0
        ident = ('V' if vehicle else 'P') + str(i // 2 + 1)
        speed = (vehicle_speed if vehicle else pedestrian_speed) * (1+rng.uniform(-speed_jitter,speed_jitter))
        spec = AgentSpec(ident, 'vehicle' if vehicle else 'pedestrian',
                         4.5 if vehicle else 0.5, 1.8 if vehicle else 0.6, speed,
                         1.5 if vehicle else 1.2, 3.0 if vehicle else 2.0,
                         vehicle_lateral_speed if vehicle else 0.7, 0.4 if vehicle else 1.0,
                         1, 1, 0.0, 1500 if vehicle else 70, encounter_speed=1.0)
        x, y = radius*math.cos(theta), radius*math.sin(theta)
        agents.append(AgentState(spec,x,y,speed))
        headings[ident], goals[ident] = theta+math.pi, (-x,-y)
    return RadialScenario(agents,headings,goals,c,radius,passing_offset,goal_tolerance,seed,
                          {'type':'circle_crossing','pairs':pairs,'vehicle_lateral_speed':vehicle_lateral_speed,
                           'vehicle_speed':vehicle_speed,'speed_jitter':speed_jitter,'rotation':rotation,
                           'goal':'antipodal_point','orientation':'fixed_initial_inward_heading',
                           'interaction':'original_policy_in_agent_local_frame','walls':False})


def world_velocity(a, headings):
    e,n = basis(headings[a.spec.id])
    return (a.speed*e[0]+a.vy*n[0], a.speed*e[1]+a.vy*n[1])


def local_control(a, agents, scene, policy, settings):
    gx,gy = scene.goals[a.spec.id]
    if math.hypot(a.x-gx,a.y-gy) <= scene.goal_tolerance:
        return Control(max(0.0,a.speed-a.spec.max_brake*settings.dt),
                       a.vy+clip(-a.vy,-a.spec.lateral_accel*settings.dt,a.spec.lateral_accel*settings.dt),
                       'BRAKE',False)
    e,n = basis(scene.headings[a.spec.id])
    local = []
    for other in agents:
        pos = (other.x,other.y)
        velocity = world_velocity(other,scene.headings)
        other_e,_ = basis(scene.headings[other.spec.id])
        forward = dot(e,other_e)
        direction = 1 if forward > 1e-8 else -1
        spec = replace(other.spec,
                       length=2*support(other.spec,scene.headings[other.spec.id],e),
                       width=2*support(other.spec,scene.headings[other.spec.id],n),direction=direction)
        if other.spec.id == a.spec.id:
            spec = replace(a.spec,direction=1,side=1,home_y=0.0)
        # Arrived bodies remain present as stationary obstacles.
        local.append(replace(other,spec=spec,x=dot(pos,e),y=dot(pos,n),
                             speed=abs(dot(velocity,e)),vy=dot(velocity,n),done=False))
    own = next(x for x in local if x.spec.id == a.spec.id)
    # This width supplies a lateral target band, not a physical road boundary.
    width = 2*scene.passing_offset+a.spec.width+2*settings.wall_clearance
    road = Road(4*scene.radius,((0.0,width),(4*scene.radius,width)),physical_walls=False)
    result = policy.control(own,local,road,settings)
    remaining = dot(scene.goals[a.spec.id],e)-own.x
    cap = safe_speed(max(0.0,remaining-scene.goal_tolerance/3),a.spec.max_brake,settings.dt)
    low = max(0.0,a.speed-a.spec.max_brake*settings.dt)
    speed = clip(min(result.speed,cap),low,a.speed+a.spec.max_accel*settings.dt)
    return replace(result,speed=speed,braking_infeasible=result.braking_infeasible or cap < low-1e-9)


def radial_snapshot(t, agents):
    return {'time':round(t,6),'agents':[{'id':a.spec.id,'x':round(a.x,6),'y':round(a.y,6),
            'speed':round(a.speed,6),'vy':round(a.vy,6),'mode':a.mode,'done':a.done} for a in agents]}


def validate_circle(scene):
    ids = [a.spec.id for a in scene.agents]
    if len(ids) != len(set(ids)):
        raise ValueError('Duplicate ids')
    for a in scene.agents:
        if not all(math.isfinite(v) for v in (a.x,a.y,a.speed,a.vy,scene.headings[a.spec.id],*scene.goals[a.spec.id])):
            raise ValueError('Nonfinite initial state')
    for a,b in combinations(scene.agents,2):
        if swept_obb_contact(a,a,b,b,scene.headings[a.spec.id],scene.headings[b.spec.id]) is not None:
            raise ValueError('Initial oriented footprints overlap: '+a.spec.id+'/'+b.spec.id)


def simulate_circle(scene, policy=None, record=True):
    validate_circle(scene)
    policy = policy or ReciprocalPolicy()
    agents = copy.deepcopy(scene.agents)
    c = scene.settings
    trace = [radial_snapshot(0,agents)] if record else []
    events, collisions = [], set()
    t,quiet,min_gap,infeasible = 0.0,0.0,float('inf'),0
    min_radius = {a.spec.id:math.hypot(a.x,a.y) for a in agents}
    status = 'timeout'
    for step in range(1,math.ceil(c.max_time/c.dt)+1):
        dt = min(c.dt,c.max_time-t)
        controls = {a.spec.id:local_control(a,agents,scene,policy,replace(c,dt=dt)) for a in agents if not a.done}
        following = []
        for a in agents:
            if a.done:
                following.append(a)
                continue
            u = controls[a.spec.id]
            e,n = basis(scene.headings[a.spec.id])
            b = replace(a,x=a.x+dt*(u.speed*e[0]+u.vy*n[0]),y=a.y+dt*(u.speed*e[1]+u.vy*n[1]),
                        speed=u.speed,vy=u.vy,mode=u.mode,committed=u.committed)
            b.path_length += math.hypot(b.x-a.x,b.y-a.y)
            if math.hypot(b.speed,b.vy)<c.stop_speed: b.waiting_time += dt
            b.peak_decel = max(a.peak_decel,(a.speed-b.speed)/dt)
            min_radius[a.spec.id] = min(min_radius[a.spec.id],math.hypot(b.x,b.y))
            infeasible += int(u.braking_infeasible)
            following.append(b)
        for i,j in combinations(range(len(agents)),2):
            a,b,an,bn = agents[i],agents[j],following[i],following[j]
            ha,hb = scene.headings[a.spec.id],scene.headings[b.spec.id]
            contact = swept_obb_contact(a,an,b,bn,ha,hb)
            min_gap = min(min_gap,swept_gap_bound(a,an,b,bn,ha,hb))
            if contact is not None:
                pair = tuple(sorted((a.spec.id,b.spec.id)))
                collisions.add(pair)
                events.append({'time':t+contact*dt,'type':'collision','agents':pair})
        t += dt
        agents = following
        for a in agents:
            if a.done: continue
            gx,gy = scene.goals[a.spec.id]
            if math.hypot(a.x-gx,a.y-gy)<=scene.goal_tolerance and a.speed==0 and a.vy==0:
                a.done,a.mode,a.exit_time = True,'DONE',t
                # No position snapping and no instantaneous velocity reset.
                events.append({'time':t,'type':'goal_arrival','agent':a.spec.id})
        if record and step % c.record_every == 0: trace.append(radial_snapshot(t,agents))
        if collisions:
            status='collision';break
        if all(a.done for a in agents):
            status='completed';break
        live=[a for a in agents if not a.done]
        quiet = quiet+dt if all(math.hypot(a.speed,a.vy)<c.stop_speed for a in live) else 0.0
        if quiet >= c.stall_time:
            status='deadlock_policy'
            events.append({'time':t,'type':'stall','classification':status})
            break
    if record and (not trace or abs(trace[-1]['time']-t)>1e-7): trace.append(radial_snapshot(t,agents))
    passed = {kind:sum(a.done and a.spec.kind==kind for a in agents) for kind in ('pedestrian','vehicle')}
    metrics={'status':status,'observation_time_s':t,'completion_time_s':t if status=='completed' else None,
             'pedestrians_passed':passed['pedestrian'],'vehicles_passed':passed['vehicle'],
             'pedestrians_total':sum(a.spec.kind=='pedestrian' for a in agents),
             'vehicles_total':sum(a.spec.kind=='vehicle' for a in agents),
             'collision_pairs':len(collisions),'boundary_violations':0,'pair_passages':None,
             'arrived_total':sum(a.done for a in agents),'total_agent_waiting_s':sum(a.waiting_time for a in agents),
             'min_pair_gap_m':min_gap,'gap_definition':'conservative whole-step SAT lower bound; not exact Euclidean distance',
             'min_wall_gap_m':None,'safety_margins_satisfied':not collisions and min_gap>=c.lateral_clearance-c.clearance_tolerance,
             'speed_ceiling_unreachable_agent_steps':infeasible,
             'closest_radius_by_agent_m':min_radius,
             'agents':[{'id':a.spec.id,'arrival_time_s':a.exit_time,'goal_error_m':math.hypot(a.x-scene.goals[a.spec.id][0],a.y-scene.goals[a.spec.id][1]),
                        'waiting_time_s':a.waiting_time,'path_length_m':a.path_length,'peak_decel_m_s2':a.peak_decel} for a in agents]}
    return {'policy':{'name':getattr(policy,'name',type(policy).__name__),
                      'parameters':asdict(policy.parameters) if hasattr(policy,'parameters') else {}},
            'scenario':{'name':scene.name,'seed':scene.seed,'settings':asdict(c),'agents':[asdict(a.spec) for a in scene.agents],
                        'initial_states':[asdict(a) for a in scene.agents],'metadata':scene.metadata,
                        'radius':scene.radius,'headings':scene.headings,'goals':scene.goals,
                        'passing_offset':scene.passing_offset,'goal_tolerance':scene.goal_tolerance},
            'metrics':metrics,'events':events,'trace':trace}
```

### pvsim/radial_experiments.py

<!-- simfile path="pvsim/radial_experiments.py" sha256="3c4d6443363c35af3406ebe3bdcdc7e9ff50e3ed1cb5d0393d2da31600ff2e4f" -->
```python
"""Matched-seed circle-crossing experiments, independent of corridor scans."""
from pathlib import Path
from collections import defaultdict, Counter
import json
from .radial import circle_crossing
from .simulation import simulate
from .social_force import make_policy
from .export import save_result, save_replay, save_homepage
from .experiments import write_csv, mean, wilson


def designs():
    return [
        ('baseline','4人4车 · 半径12 m · 车辆不侧移',{}),
        ('mobile','4人4车 · 车辆侧移上限0.2 m/s',{'vehicle_lateral_speed':.2}),
        ('one_pair','1人1车 · 对向穿越',{'pairs':1}),
        ('two_pairs','2人2车 · 十字汇聚',{'pairs':2}),
        ('dense','6人6车 · 密集汇聚',{'pairs':6}),
        ('radius_8','4人4车 · 初始半径8 m',{'radius':8}),
        ('radius_16','4人4车 · 初始半径16 m',{'radius':16}),
        ('slow_vehicle','4人4车 · 车速基值1.5 m/s',{'vehicle_speed':1.5}),
    ]


def run_radial(directory, seeds=5):
    out=Path(directory);out.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    rows,replay=[],[]
    with (out/'runs.jsonl').open('w',encoding='utf-8') as log:
        for slug,label,params in designs():
            for seed in range(seeds):
                scene=circle_crossing(seed=seed,**params)
                for policy in ('reciprocal','social_force','sfm_guided'):
                    result=simulate(scene,make_policy(policy),record=seed==0)
                    m=result['metrics']
                    row={'case':slug,'label':label,'seed':seed,'policy':policy,'radius_m':scene.radius,
                         'pairs':scene.metadata['pairs'],'vehicle_lateral_speed_m_s':scene.metadata['vehicle_lateral_speed'],
                         'vehicle_speed_m_s':scene.metadata['vehicle_speed'],
                         **{key:m[key] for key in ('status','observation_time_s','completion_time_s','arrived_total',
                            'pedestrians_passed','vehicles_passed','collision_pairs','min_pair_gap_m',
                            'total_agent_waiting_s','safety_margins_satisfied')}}
                    rows.append(row)
                    log.write(json.dumps({'design':{'case':slug,'params':params},'result':dict(result,trace=[])},ensure_ascii=False)+'\n')
                    if seed==0:
                        save_result(result,out/'trajectories'/(slug+'_'+policy))
                        replay.append((label+' · '+policy,result))
            print(f'circle crossing {slug}: {seeds} seeds x 3 policies',flush=True)
    groups=defaultdict(list)
    for row in rows:groups[(row['case'],row['policy'])].append(row)
    summary=[]
    for (case,policy),rs in groups.items():
        successes=sum(r['status']=='completed' for r in rs)
        lo,hi=wilson(successes,len(rs))
        summary.append({'case':case,'label':rs[0]['label'],'policy':policy,'runs':len(rs),
                        'completion_rate':successes/len(rs),'wilson_low':lo,'wilson_high':hi,
                        'completion_mean_s':mean([r['completion_time_s'] for r in rs if r['completion_time_s'] is not None]),
                        'mean_arrived':mean([r['arrived_total'] for r in rs]),
                        'collision_runs':sum(r['collision_pairs']>0 for r in rs),
                        'mean_waiting_s':mean([r['total_agent_waiting_s'] for r in rs]),
                        'min_gap_lower_bound_m':min(r['min_pair_gap_m'] for r in rs),
                        'status_counts':json.dumps(dict(Counter(r['status'] for r in rs)),ensure_ascii=False)})
    write_csv(out/'runs.csv',rows);write_csv(out/'summary.csv',summary)
    (out/'summary.json').write_text(json.dumps(summary,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
    save_replay(replay,out/'replay.html')
    if out.resolve()==Path('results/circle_crossing').resolve():
        save_homepage(out/'replay.html',Path.cwd())
    return rows,summary
```

### pvsim/radial_geometry.py

<!-- simfile path="pvsim/radial_geometry.py" sha256="adedf215343b2788176a8d7373aed57dce0f407d9cd942e61feeb389e4db4ef7" -->
```python
"""Fixed-heading oriented rectangles; continuous SAT for linear translations."""
import math


def basis(angle):
    return (math.cos(angle), math.sin(angle)), (-math.sin(angle), math.cos(angle))


def dot(a, b):
    return a[0] * b[0] + a[1] * b[1]


def support(spec, angle, axis):
    e, n = basis(angle)
    return abs(dot(e, axis)) * spec.length / 2 + abs(dot(n, axis)) * spec.width / 2


def axes(ha, hb):
    return (*basis(ha), *basis(hb))


def projected_gap(a, b, ha, hb):
    """SAT separation lower bound, not exact Euclidean clearance."""
    delta = (a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y)
    return max(abs(dot(delta, axis)) - support(a.spec, ha, axis) - support(b.spec, hb, axis)
               for axis in axes(ha, hb))


def swept_obb_contact(a, an, b, bn, ha, hb):
    enter, leave = 0.0, 1.0
    delta = (a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y)
    change = (an.x - bn.x - delta[0], an.y - bn.y - delta[1])
    for axis in axes(ha, hb):
        z, dz = dot(delta, axis), dot(change, axis)
        radius = support(a.spec, ha, axis) + support(b.spec, hb, axis)
        if abs(dz) < 1e-12:
            if abs(z) > radius:
                return None
        else:
            lo, hi = sorted(((-radius - z) / dz, (radius - z) / dz))
            enter, leave = max(enter, lo), min(leave, hi)
            if enter > leave:
                return None
    return max(0.0, enter) if enter <= 1 and leave >= 0 else None


def swept_gap_bound(a, an, b, bn, ha, hb):
    """Conservative whole-step clearance: starting SAT gap minus relative travel."""
    distance = math.hypot((an.x-a.x)-(bn.x-b.x), (an.y-a.y)-(bn.y-b.y))
    return max(0.0, projected_gap(a, b, ha, hb) - distance)
```
